We prove that the batch-cutting variant inherits the convergence rate $O(k^{2/(1-q)})$ of the standard algorithm, where $k$ is the number of outer iterations and $q$ is the number of objectives. Computational experiments on eight test problems with $q \in \{2,3,4,5\}$ show that parallelism on 8 cores \revise{increases the speed by a factor of 1.1 to 4.2}, and batch cutting consistently reduces the iteration count by 62--80\%. However, the wall-clock benefit of batch cutting is problem-dependent: the additional cuts per iteration accelerate vertex count growth, so batch cutting is most effective when per-vertex subproblem cost dominates.

</details>


### [111] [Convergence rate of the Halpern iterations with possibly distinct anchor and initial guess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05630)
*Jianing He,Qiao-Li Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Halpern迭代中锚点与初始猜测不同时的收敛速度，给出了预定和自适应锚定参数的紧收敛率估计，推广了现有工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Halpern迭代的收敛率估计仅适用于锚点与初始猜测相同的特殊情况，本文旨在推广到一般情况，即锚点与初始猜测可以不同。

Method: 通过分析预定和自适应两种锚定参数设置下的Halpern迭代，推导出相应的收敛率估计，并证明这些估计是紧的。

Result: 给出了预定和自适应锚定参数的紧收敛率估计，推广了现有相关工作的结果。

Conclusion: 本文的收敛率估计适用于锚点与初始猜测不同的更一般情形，为Halpern迭代的实际应用提供了更广泛的理论保证。

Abstract: All existing convergence rate estimates of Halpern iterations are established only for the case where the anchor coincides with the initial guess. This paper aims to investigate the convergence rate of general Halpern iterations, where the anchor and the initial guess may not necessarily be the same. We present tight convergence rate estimates for both predetermined and adaptive anchoring parameters. These results generalize existing related work.

</details>


### [112] [Uniqueness and stability of Lagrange multipliers and associated qualification conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06067)
*Patrick Mehlitz,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究抽象空间中约束优化问题拉格朗日乘子的唯一性与稳定性，探讨了严格Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz条件与拉格朗日乘子映射孤立平静性之间的关系，并证明该条件在某些假设下等价于严格的RZK条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究抽象空间中约束优化问题拉格朗日乘子的唯一性和稳定性，这是优化理论中的基础性问题。

Method: 通过分析严格Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz条件与拉格朗日乘子映射的孤立平静性之间的关系，在附加假设下证明了二者的等价性，并给出反例说明假设的必要性。

Result: 证明了孤立平静性条件是RZK约束规格的充分条件，并在附加假设下等价于严格RZK条件。

Conclusion: 严格RZK条件蕴含拉格朗日乘子映射的孤立平静性，后者保证乘子的唯一性；在某些条件下二者等价。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with uniqueness and stability of Lagrange multipliers for constrained optimization problems in abstract spaces. It is well known that validity of the strict Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz condition implies the so-called isolated calmness, a one-sided Lipschitz property tailored for set-valued mappings, of some Lagrange multiplier mapping associated with a perturbed version of the original optimization problem, and the latter indeed is enough to guarantee uniqueness of the Lagrange multiplier. The paper studies the isolated calmness of the Lagrange multiplier mapping in detail. Exemplary, it is shown that this condition is sufficient for the Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz constraint qualification and, in the presence of additional assumptions, even equivalent to the strict Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz condition. Illustrative examples are presented to underline the necessity of postulated assumptions.

</details>


### [113] [Second order splitting dynamics for stochastic monotone inclusions with closed loop distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06280)
*Wutao Si,Hamza Ennaji,Jalal Fadili*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了希尔伯特空间中极大单调算子与余强制算子之和的零点问题，该公式可建模决策依赖分布的随机优化（即执行预测问题）。提出了基于分布评估的前向后向分裂算子的连续时间二阶动力学，在一般一致单调性假设下证明了平衡点的存在唯一性，并通过消失粘性阻尼系数实现了轨迹强收敛与快速渐近速度；在强单调情形下，采用恒定Polyak型阻尼系数获得了全局指数收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解极大单调算子与余强制算子之和的零点问题，该形式可自然捕捉具有决策依赖分布的随机优化问题（即执行预测），从而统一处理这类具有分布偏移特性的优化场景。

Method: 提出了连续时间二阶动力学系统，采用分布评估的前向后向分裂算子；通过消失粘性阻尼系数实现轨迹强收敛，并在强单调条件下引入恒定Polyak型阻尼系数。

Result: 在一般一致单调性假设下，证明了平衡点的存在唯一性，并建立了轨迹的强收敛性及速度的快速渐近收敛率；在强单调设定下，获得了全局指数收敛率。

Conclusion: 所提出的连续时间二阶动力学方法能有效解决执行预测问题中的零点求解，具备强收敛保证和快速收敛速度，为决策依赖分布下的优化提供了理论框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the problem of finding a zero of the sum of a maximal monotone operator $A$ and a cocoercive operator $\Bm$ in a Hilbert space. This formulation naturally captures stochastic optimization problems with decision-dependent distributions, often referred to as performative prediction. We propose and analyze continuous-time second-order dynamics governed by a distributionally evaluated forward-backward splitting operator. We establish the existence and uniqueness of the equilibrium point under a general uniform monotonicity assumption. In this setting, employing a vanishing viscous damping coefficient, we prove the strong convergence of the trajectories to the equilibrium, accompanied by fast asymptotic convergence rates for the velocities. Furthermore, when the regularizing operator is strongly monotone, we consider a constant Polyak-type damping coefficient and we establish global exponential convergence rates for the dynamical system.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [HyFAD: Hybrid Time-Frequency Diffusion with Frequency-Aware Embedding for Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05239)
*Hongfan Gao,Wangmeng Shen,Bin Yang,Jilin Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: HyFAD是一种混合时频扩散模型，通过时域-频域级联去噪和频率感知步长嵌入，在时间序列插补任务上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在时间序列建模中面临频率敏感去噪困难、高频重建不佳以及全局趋势与局部动态平衡不足等问题。

Method: 基于DDPM范式，提出耦合时频扩散框架：时域扩散捕获低频全局趋势，频域扩散细化高频谱分量；引入频率感知步长嵌入，建立扩散步长与频谱分量的关系，提供步长依赖的频谱指导。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: HyFAD通过时频域级联去噪与频率感知嵌入有效解决了现有模型在频率敏感性和多尺度建模上的不足，实现了从粗到细的时间序列生成。

Abstract: Diffusion models have demonstrated strong performance in time series modeling due to their ability to progressively capture complex data distributions through iterative denoising. However, existing approaches struggle with frequency-sensitive denoising, high-frequency reconstruction and balancing global trends with local dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HyFAD}, a \textbf{Hy}brid time-frequency \textbf{D}iffusion model with \textbf{F}requency-\textbf{A}ware embedding for time series imputation. Built upon the DDPM paradigm, HyFAD adopts a coupled time-frequency diffusion framework, in which the reverse denoising proceeds sequentially from the time domain to the frequency domain, enabling coarse-to-fine generation. Specifically, the time-domain diffusion process captures low-frequency global trends, while the frequency-domain diffusion process refines high-frequency spectral components. We further introduce a frequency-aware step embedding that exploits the relationship between diffusion steps and spectral components, providing step-dependent spectral guidance and facilitates more accurate band-wise reconstruction. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that HyFAD achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our source code is available at https://github.com/hongfangao/HyFAD.

</details>


### [115] [Deterministic Envelopes for Tamed SGLD: Decoupling Stochastic-Gradient Noise and Localizing Taming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05242)
*Yiwei Zhou,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机梯度Langevin算法中依赖同一随机梯度实现的分母会引入稳态偏差的问题，提出了基于确定性包络的结构保持框架来设计分母，避免了偏差同时保留了稳定性，并分析了混合策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机梯度Langevin算法使用驯化分母稳定非全局Lipschitz漂移时，若分母与分子依赖同一随机梯度实现，即使原始梯度无偏也会产生稳态偏差。作者旨在消除这一偏差同时保持稳定性。

Method: 提出结构保持框架：在采样Oracle噪声前固定分母，使用局部确定性包络避免在典型区域不必要的驯化；进一步分析远尾条件，提出混合核（典型区域软包络+罕见漂移硬尾控制）。

Result: 理论分析了稳态误差分解为Oracle依赖驯化导致的偏差与确定性稳定化引入的剩余误差；实验验证了随机分母的稳态畸变、确定性包络设计的偏差减少以及混合构造的稳定效果。

Conclusion: 确定性包络框架能有效消除驯化分母的偏差，混合策略在典型区域和罕见漂移场景下均能兼顾偏差与稳定性。

Abstract: Stochastic-gradient Langevin algorithms often use tamed denominators to stabilize non-globally Lipschitz drifts. This paper shows that when the denominator depends on the same stochastic-gradient realization as the numerator, the taming step changes the stochastic oracle itself and can create a stationary bias even if the original stochastic gradient is unbiased. We propose a structure-preserving framework for designing tamed denominators. It fixes the denominator before the oracle noise is sampled and uses localized deterministic envelopes to avoid unnecessary taming in typical regions. These kernels keep the stabilizing effect of taming while avoiding the bias introduced by a gradient-dependent denominator. Our theory explains how the stationary error splits into the bias caused by oracle-dependent taming and the remaining error introduced by deterministic stabilization. Within this deterministic-envelope family, the analysis identifies a far-tail condition that explains the limitation of local soft envelopes and motivates a hybrid member: soft in the typical region, but protected by hard-tail control on rare excursions. Experiments confirm the predicted stationary distortions of random denominators, the bias reduction of deterministic-envelope designs, and the stabilizing effect of the hybrid construction.

</details>


### [116] [Harnessing Source Heterogeneity for Cluster-Structured Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05258)
*Xiaohui Yin,Jun Jin,Shane J. Sacco,Robert H. Aseltine,Kun Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Trans-GLMC，一种面向广义线性模型的聚类结构迁移学习方法，通过恢复源数据中潜在的聚类结构，实现有差异的迁移学习，并以CHIME医院自杀风险预测数据为例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标人群数据有限但存在多个辅助源时，传统迁移学习将源选择简化为二元（有用/无用）决策，忽视了源之间可能存在的结构化、聚类式的差异性迁移能力。在CHIME医院自杀风险研究中，各医院数据稀疏导致单医院模型不稳定，而简单合并又会掩盖医院间的异质性。

Method: Trans-GLMC首先基于系数距离构建目标与候选源之间的距离度量，恢复潜在的源聚类结构；然后结合全局融合、聚类内部精炼和目标去偏三个步骤，生成适应检测到结构的估计量。

Result: 建立了非渐近误差界：当存在有意义的目标聚类时，所提方法优于非聚类方法；否则达到与非聚类方法相同的速率（仅常数项差异）。在模拟实验和CHIME研究中，Trans-GLMC提高了设施特定的预测精度，识别出具有互迁移性的可解释医院社区，并恢复了临床一致的自杀风险因素。

Conclusion: Trans-GLMC通过聚类结构迁移学习有效解决了源异构性下的迁移学习问题，在实际应用中既提升了预测性能，又提供了可解释的聚类结构。

Abstract: Transfer learning is a natural strategy when a target population has limited data but multiple related auxiliary sources are available. A central difficulty is source heterogeneity: auxiliary sources may not be equally useful, and their usefulness may vary in a structured, cluster-like fashion. Existing transfer-learning methods often reduce source selection to a binary informative/non-informative decision, overlooking subgroups of sources with differential transferability. Motivated by a suicide-risk study using data from the Connecticut Hospital Information Management Exchange (CHIME), comprising 636,758 patients across 27 hospitals, we propose Trans-GLMC, a cluster-structured transfer-learning procedure for generalized linear models. The CHIME setting illustrates the core challenge: hospital-specific risk models are unstable because suicide attempts are rare at any single facility, whereas indiscriminate pooling across hospitals can obscure facility-level differences in patient mix and risk profiles. Trans-GLMC first constructs a coefficient-based distance among the target and candidate sources to recover latent source clusters. It then combines global fusion, within-cluster refinement, and target debiasing to produce an estimator that adapts to the detected structure. We establish a non-asymptotic error bound that improves over its unclustered counterpart whenever a meaningful target cluster exists and matches the unclustered rate up to constants otherwise. In simulations and in the CHIME study, Trans-GLMC improves facility-specific prediction, identifies interpretable communities of hospitals with mutual transferability, and recovers clinically coherent suicide-risk factors.

</details>


### [117] [TabSODA: Tabular Diffusion based Imputation with Skip Pattern Detection and Ordinal Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05361)
*Yuyu Chen,Taehyo Kim,Hai Shu,Yang Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TabSODA是一种基于EM的扩散插补方法，通过结构跳题检测和序数感知机制，在大规模调查数据缺失插补中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格扩散方法无法有效处理大规模调查中的两个问题：一是结构跳题（设计性不适用项）常被误当作无回答进行插补；二是序数变量被当作名义变量处理（使用one-hot或analog-bit编码），丢失了有序信息。

Method: 基于EDM框架，提出TabSODA方法：1) 通过EM算法在去噪损失和反向采样过程中传播结构跳题信息；2) 对序数变量使用累积probit标量潜变量表示，对名义变量保留analog-bit编码；3) 若已有编码表跳题掩码则直接使用，否则通过CART跳题模式挖掘器（TabSODA+SKIP变体）从原始响应和问卷顺序中估计掩码。

Result: 在PATH和NSDUH两项美国全国代表性调查上，TabSODA在序数MACE上降低最高达23.7%，分类准确率提升最高达9%（超越最强基线，覆盖MCAR/MAR/MNAR缺失机制）。跳题挖掘器在两个数据集上达到近乎完美的精确度，使TabSODA+SKIP的表现接近使用编码表掩码的版本。

Conclusion: TabSODA有效解决了大规模调查中结构跳题和序数感知两个关键挑战，且无需编码表即可通过自动挖掘实现近似的插补性能，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Missing data imputation in large-scale surveys faces two challenges that are not well handled by current tabular diffusion methods. First, \emph{structural skips}, cells made inapplicable by questionnaire design, should not be imputed but are often conflated with item nonresponse. Second, \emph{ordinal} responses encode ordered categories, yet most pipelines treat them as nominal levels through one-hot or analog-bit encodings. We introduce \textbf{TabSODA} (\textbf{Tab}ular diffusion with \textbf{S}kip pattern detection and \textbf{O}r\textbf{d}inal \textbf{A}wareness), an Expectation-Maximization (EM)-based diffusion imputer built on the Elucidated Diffusion Model (EDM) framework. TabSODA propagates structural skips through the denoising loss and reverse-time sampler, and represents ordinal variables with cumulative-probit scalar latents while retaining analog-bit encodings for nominal variables. When a codebook skip mask is available, TabSODA uses it directly; otherwise, the TabSODA+SKIP variant estimates the mask from raw responses and questionnaire order using a CART-based skip-pattern miner. On Population Assessment of Tobacco and Health (PATH) study and the National Survey on Drug Use and Health (NSDUH), two nationally representative U.S.\ surveys, TabSODA reduces ordinal MACE by up to $23.7\%$ and improves categorical accuracy by up to $9\%$ over the strongest baseline across MCAR, MAR, and MNAR masking. The skip miner achieves near-perfect precision on both datasets, allowing TabSODA+SKIP to closely track the codebook-mask variant.

</details>


### [118] [Environment-Robust Representation Learning with Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05365)
*Yuli Slavutsky,Matthew Shen,Bohan Wu,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多环境预测的贝叶斯模型，假设环境改变潜在变量的分布，而观测变量和目标的生成机制在条件独立下保持稳定。通过变分推断和实证贝叶斯方法，模型分解为环境内项和跨环境平衡项，在多个真实场景（天文、微生物、ICU脓毒症）中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多环境预测中环境分布偏移问题，利用潜在变量在不同环境间保持稳定条件关系的假设，提高模型在新环境中的泛化能力。

Method: 构建贝叶斯模型，假设环境改变潜在变量分布但条件生成机制稳定；推导变分目标函数，分解为环境内项和跨环境平衡项；采用经验贝叶斯设定先验；提出摊销变分算法进行后验近似，利用学习到的潜在变量在新环境中进行预测。

Result: 在天文源识别、微生物组疾病检测和ICU脓毒症预测三个真实场景中，该方法均优于现有方法在新环境中的预测性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模环境间潜在变量的分布变化并保持条件生成机制不变，该方法有效提升了跨环境泛化能力，验证了模型在多领域实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: We consider multi-environment prediction problems. We assume the environments change the distribution of a latent variable, while the mechanisms generating observed covariates and targets remain stable conditional on that variable. For example, hospitals or clinical cohorts may differ in the prevalence of latent patient states, even though the relationships between those states, physiological measurements, and outcomes remain unchanged. Given a dataset from multiple environments, we formulate a Bayesian model for such problems and derive the corresponding variational objective. We show that this objective decomposes into per-environment terms and an additional cross-environment balancing term induced by the model's structure. We use an empirical Bayes method to set the prior and incorporate it into the objective. Based on this objective, we develop an amortized variational algorithm for posterior approximation, and use the resulting learned latent variables to form predictions in new environments.We study our approach through simulations and real-world studies of astronomical source identification, microbiome-based disease detection, and ICU sepsis prediction. Across these settings, our method outperforms previous approaches for prediction in new environments.

</details>


### [119] [Sparse Functional Singular Value Decomposition for Biclustering and Triclustering Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05488)
*Yue Zhao,Thierry Chekouo,Sandra Safo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Tri-SfSVD框架，一种统一的稀疏函数奇异值分解方法，用于从纵向高维稀疏不规则观测数据中发现双聚类和三聚类结构，无需数据插值或形状同质性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统(双)聚类和函数型数据分析方法难以处理纵向组学数据的高维性、稀疏采样和不规则观测问题，现有函数型双聚类方法依赖临时插值或施加严格的形状同质性假设，缺乏统一且灵活的解决方案。

Method: Tri-SfSVD将连续轨迹估计与样本、特征、时间维度的同时选择整合到单一优化框架中，通过施加稀疏惩罚实现主体、变量和时间子区域的同时选择，直接在观测数据上发现局部化结构。

Result: 模拟实验表明Tri-SfSVD在高维设置下优于现有方法。在IBD多组学数据中识别出三个双聚类，将样本簇与IBD相关临床特征关联到特定细菌分类群对应的微生物通路群；在多通道EEG数据中识别出三个三聚类，将样本簇与酒精相关表型关联到局部脑活动模式。

Conclusion: Tri-SfSVD为纵向数据中的双聚类和三聚类发现提供了统一有效的框架，能够直接处理稀疏不规则观测数据，生成可解释的样本-特征关联，有助于刻画疾病异质性。

Abstract: Identifying subtypes of complex conditions, such as Inflammatory Bowel Disease (IBD), often requires capturing latent patterns in longitudinal omics data. However, these data are typically high-dimensional, sparsely sampled, and irregularly observed over time, posing substantial challenges for conventional (bi)clustering and functional data analysis methods. We propose Tri-SfSVD, a unified sparse functional Singular Value Decomposition framework for discovering biclusters and triclusters in longitudinal data. Unlike existing functional biclustering methods that rely on ad hoc imputation or enforce restrictive shape-homogeneity assumptions, Tri-SfSVD integrates continuous trajectory estimation with simultaneous subject, feature, and temporal selection within a single optimization framework. By imposing sparse penalties across subjects, variables, and temporal subregions, the proposed method works directly on observed data to uncover localized structures at the subject, subject-feature, and subject-feature-time levels. Extensive simulations demonstrate that Tri-SfSVD outperforms existing approaches in high-dimensional settings. Applied to IBD multi-omics data, the method identified three biclusters linking sample clusters with distinct IBD-related clinical characteristics to microbial pathway groups associated with specific bacterial taxa, providing interpretable subject-pathway associations for characterizing disease heterogeneity. Applied to multi-channel EEG data, the method identified three triclusters linking sample clusters with distinct alcohol-related phenotypes to localized brain activity patterns, including subgroup differences separated by temporal subregions within the same spatial region.

</details>


### [120] [Conformal Risk-Averse Decision Making with Action Conditional Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05551)
*Zihan Zhu,Shayan Kiyani,George Pappas. Hamed Hassani*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出动作条件共形预测方法，将共形预测的条件安全保障从边际水平提升到动作条件水平，并通过pinball损失最小化算法实现有限样本下的风险厌恶决策优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形预测仅提供边际安全保障，无法满足需要对每个动作都提供明确风险保证的决策场景需求。

Method: (i) 提出动作条件共形预测，针对决策者每个动作提供明确的安全保证；(ii) 证明动作条件预测集可作为风险厌恶决策者优化条件风险价值时的可行决策空间代理；(iii) 提出基于pinball损失最小化的有限样本算法，将Gibbs等人的框架与动作条件保证连接。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在动作条件性能上显著优于共形基线方法。

Conclusion: 动作条件共形预测能够为风险厌恶决策提供更精细、更可靠的安全保障，弥补了边际保证的不足。

Abstract: Reliable decision making pipelines powered by machine learning models require uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods that come with explicit safety guarantees. Conformal prediction provides such UQ by wrapping ML predictions into prediction sets, and recent work by Kiyani et al. (2025b) established that these sets can be translated into optimal risk-averse decision policies -- yet only inheriting marginal safety guarantees. We generalize and strengthen their results by (i) introducing action-conditional conformal prediction, which yields safety guarantees conditioned explicitly on each action taken by the decision maker, (ii) showing that action-conditional prediction sets serve as a proxy for the feasible decision space for risk-averse decision makers aiming to optimize action-conditional value-at-risk, and (iii) proposing a principled finite-sample algorithm based on pinball-loss minimization, connecting the framework of Gibbs et al. (2025) to action-conditional guarantees. Experiments on two real-world datasets confirm that our approach significantly improves action-conditional performance over conformal baselines.

</details>


### [121] [Finding Most Influential Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05919)
*Lucas D. Konrad,Nikolas Kuschnig*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将最大影响集（MIS）选择问题——即寻找移除后最大程度改变目标估计量的大小为k的子集——从指数级搜索复杂度降低到O(n)每轮迭代的算法。核心思想是对于具有线性分式留出集效应的估计量，MIS选择可转化为单参数top-k问题序列，并通过Dinkelbach方法实现有限步终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别最具影响力的k元子集（MIS）在组合上不可行（需搜索C(n,k)个子集），亟需高效算法。

Method: 将MIS选择问题转化为单参数序列的top-k问题；利用Dinkelbach方法迭代求解，每轮仅需O(n)复杂度且有限步终止。

Result: 对于固定残差化输入，算法返回单变量比率目标的全局最优集；在估计 nuisance 函数下，满足均匀分母和生成得分稳定性可逼近一阶正交得分目标，分离条件下可精确恢复集合。

Conclusion: 该方法能够恢复先前因计算不可行而无法获得的精确MIS，模拟和实际应用验证了有效性。

Abstract: Identifying most influential sets (MIS) - size-$k$ subsets whose removal maximally changes a target estimand - is typically infeasible because it requires searching over $\binom{n}{k}$ subsets. For estimands with linear-fractional leave-set-out effects, we show that MIS selection reduces to a one-parameter sequence of top-$k$ problems. Dinkelbach's method yields an algorithm with $\mathcal{O}(n)$ cost per iteration and finite termination. For fixed residualized inputs, the algorithm returns a globally optimal set for the univariate ratio objective, including the oracle-residualized partial linear model. With estimated nuisance functions, uniform denominator and generated-score stability imply approximation to the first-order oracle orthogonal-score objective; exact set recovery follows under a separation condition. Simulations and applications show that the method recovers exact MIS that were previously computationally inaccessible.

</details>


### [122] [EML-CD: Causal Mechanism Recovery via EML Symbolic Trees in Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05942)
*Sota Asanuma*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出EML-CD框架，通过门控EML二叉树表示因果机制，从神经网络权重中提取可解释的闭式因果方程，在多个数据集上验证了机制恢复能力，结构恢复性能与主流方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于神经网络的非线性因果发现方法虽能恢复DAG结构，但每个因果机制仍是黑箱；Waxman等人指出从NN权重提取因果机制是不适定问题。因此需要一种既能进行因果结构学习，又能同时恢复可解释因果机制的方法。

Method: 提出EML-CD框架，核心是将EML算子（通过单一二元算子组合初等函数）集成到因果结构学习中。将每个边机制表示为门控EML二叉树，自动发现闭式因果方程，可直接从输出方程计算解析雅可比矩阵。

Result: 在Sachs蛋白质信号数据（d=11）上SHD=11.2±0.4，与PC/GES相当，低于CAM；检测边精度0.756、召回0.365。双变量测试中恢复10/11初等函数族（保留形状相关性≥0.96）。符号合成数据上机制f-MSE均值3.67远低于固定SINDy字典的7644。Causal Chambers子集上深度2模型F1=0.444优于线性OLS-BIC的0.273。

Conclusion: EML-CD能够从数据中发现带有闭式方程的可解释因果机制，同时保持与主流方法相当的因果结构恢复性能，在机制恢复的准确性和可解释性方面显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Neural network (NN)-based nonlinear causal discovery methods recover DAG structure but leave each causal mechanism as a black box. Waxman et al. argued that extracting causal mechanisms from NN weights is ill-posed. We propose EML-CD, a framework that integrates the EML operator (capable of composing elementary functions from a single binary operator) into causal structure learning, with interpretable mechanism recovery as the primary objective. EML-CD represents each edge mechanism as a gated EML binary tree and automatically discovers closed-form causal equations. Analytical Jacobians can be directly computed from the output equations, enabling quantitative understanding of causal effects. On real data (Sachs protein signaling, d=11), EML-CD achieves SHD=11.2 +/- 0.4 (5-seed mean; baselines are single deterministic runs), on par with PC/GES within seed variance and below CAM, while attaching closed-form equations to each detected edge (precision 0.756, recall 0.365). In a controlled bivariate test with known mechanisms, EML-CD recovers 10 of 11 elementary function families faithfully (held-out shape correlation >= 0.96; only high-frequency sine is partial). On a symbolic synthetic benchmark, EML-CD attains a substantially lower and more stable held-out mechanism f-MSE than a fixed SINDy dictionary (mean 3.67 vs. 7644, the latter inflated by catastrophic extrapolation on one seed), although its structure recovery (SHD 14.0) only matches the dictionary and stays below specialized optimizers; on the Causal Chambers light-tunnel subset, a depth-2 model improves F1 over linear OLS-BIC (0.444 vs. 0.273).

</details>


### [123] [Fast and Robust Convergence Rate for TD(0) with Linear Function Approximation, Universal Learning Steps and I.I.D. Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05967)
*Ziad Kobeissi,Éloïse Berthier*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究带线性函数逼近的TD(0)算法在有限时间内的收敛行为，建立了均方误差的O(1/k)收敛率，该速率具有最优迭代依赖、对病态条件鲁棒且不依赖于最小特征值，并提出了改进变体PCTD(0)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TD(0)的O(1/k)收敛率均依赖于线性参数化非中心协方差矩阵的最小特征值，导致收敛速度受病态条件影响严重。本文旨在建立一种不依赖该最小特征值、对病态条件鲁棒且具有最优迭代阶数的收敛率。

Method: 采用on-policy独立同分布样本、恒定学习步长和Polyak-Juditsky平均方法；在强混合马尔可夫链假设下提出PCTD(0)变体以改善收敛性质。

Result: 建立了均方误差的O(1/k)收敛率，该速率仅依赖于初始误差和模型无关常数，与最小特征值无关，且乘法常数小于11。PCTD(0)在强混合假设下具有更好的收敛性质。

Conclusion: TD(0)在LFA下可获得不依赖最小特征值的O(1/k)最优收敛率，PCTD(0)进一步提升了马尔可夫链场景下的性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the finite-time behavior of the TD(0) temporal-difference method with linear function approximation (LFA). We consider on-policy independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) samples, a constant learning step, and the Polyak-Juditsky averaging method. We establish a new convergence rate, for the Mean-Square Error (MSE) on the approximated function, that is (i) fast in the sense that it admits an optimal dependency in the number of iterations k (i.e., of order 1/k), (ii) robust to ill-conditioning: it only depends on an initial error and modelindependent constants and (iii) sharp up to a multiplicative constant lower than 11. In particular, it does not depend on the smallest eigenvalue of the uncentered covariance matrix of the linear parametrization, unlike all pre-existing O(1/k) rates in the TD(0) literature. We also introduce PCTD(0), a variant of TD(0), which benefits from better convergence properties under an additional assumption of strong mixing on the Markov Chain.

</details>


### [124] [Adaptive Learning Rates with Surrogate Probability for Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06043)
*Jongyeong Lee,Junya Honda,Shinji Ito,Chansoo Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对FTPL算法提出了基于替代概率函数的自适应学习率，实现了Pareto扰动下任意α>1的best-of-both-worlds保证，并推广到专家建议的bandit问题，在保持计算简洁性的同时实现了概率相关的自适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FTPL作为FTRL的计算高效替代方案，由于其无优化性质导致概率依赖的自适应学习率设计困难，现有的BOBW保证仅限于特定参数（α=2）。

Method: 引入替代概率函数，仅利用可获得的量计算，无需精确概率；基于此设计自适应学习率，适用于Pareto扰动的FTPL（任意α>1）及专家建议bandit问题。

Result: 为FTPL在Pareto扰动下任意α>1提供了BOBW保证，推广了先前仅限于α=2的结果；并在专家建议的bandit问题中也实现了BOBW保证。

Conclusion: 提出的替代概率方法在保持FTPL计算简洁性的同时实现了概率相关的自适应性，该方法论可能对其他算法框架和学习率设计具有独立价值。

Abstract: Follow-the-regularized-leader framework has shown effectiveness and flexibility in online learning problems, where the choice of learning rates are known to be crucial. Recently, adaptive learning rates defined in terms of the arm-selection probabilities, obtained by solving convex optimization, have achieved improved best-of-both-worlds (BOBW) guarantees in various bandit problems. In contrast, BOBW guarantees for its computationally efficient alternative, follow-the-perturbed-leader (FTPL), remain relatively limited since its optimization-free nature ironically makes the design of adaptive, probability-dependent learning rates non-trivial. To address this challenge, we propose an adaptive learning rate for FTPL by introducing surrogate probability functions that can be computed only from the available quantities, without requiring the exact probabilities. Based on these learning rates with surrogate functions, we provide the BOBW guarantee for FTPL with Pareto perturbations for any shape parameter $α>1$, generalizing prior results restricted to specific choices of $α=2$. We further show the BOBW guarantees for FTPL with adaptive learning rates in the bandit problem with expert advices. Our approach preserves the computational simplicity of FTPL while enabling probability-dependent adaptivity, and the surrogate-based methodology may be of independent interest in other algorithmic frameworks beyond FTPL and learning rate designs.

</details>


### [125] [Effective Dimensionality as an Operator Invariant for Physics-Preserving Constraint Adaptation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06171)
*Cornelius Otchere,Michael Shields*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用Fisher信息矩阵量化物理约束模型的有效自由度（d_eff），证明对于有限维核的微分算子，d_eff收敛于核维度，成为连续算子的结构不变量；对于无限维核算子，d_eff衡量网络对核的有限维表示带宽。基于此，提出子空间投影策略，将参数更新投影到预训练物理算子的零空间，在不破坏已学物理规律的前提下快速适应新边界条件，无需从头训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）由于共享参数空间同时拟合控制微分方程和边界条件，固有问题干扰。现有方法缺乏对参数空间中物理约束与数据约束之间结构性冲突的量化分析工具。

Method: 1) 利用Fisher信息矩阵定义并计算有效自由度d_eff，量化微分算子对参数方向的约束程度；2) 理论证明对于有限维核算子，d_eff收敛于核维度，与网络架构无关；3) 提出子空间投影策略，将边界适应过程中的参数更新投影到预训练物理算子的零空间，确保新边界条件被满足的同时不干扰已学习的物理规律。

Result: d_eff对于有限维核算子精确收敛于核维度，成为结构不变量；对于无限维核算子反映网络的有限维表示带宽。子空间投影方法在数秒至数分钟内达到与梯度微调相近的适应质量，而梯度方法需要更多时间和调参。

Conclusion: d_eff可作为先验结构诊断工具，将其驱动至零可验证问题适定性。子空间投影策略为PINNs的边界条件适应提供了一种高效、无需从头训练的替代方案。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks inherently suffer from task interference because they rely on a shared parameter space to satisfy both governing differential equations and boundary conditions. We analyze this structural conflict using the Fisher Information Matrix to quantify the effective degrees of freedom ($d_{eff}$) in a physics-constrained model. Unlike the classical $d_{eff}$ which measures how many parameter directions are informed by data against a statistical prior, our $d_{eff}$ measures the dimension of the parameter directions unconstrained by the differential operator. For operators with finite-dimensional kernel, we show that $d_{eff}$ converges to the kernel dimension exactly, independent of network width, depth, or activation function, recasting it from a fit diagnostic into a structural invariant of the underlying continuous operator. For operators with infinite-dimensional kernel, $d_{eff}$ instead measures the network's finite-dimensional representational bandwidth for that kernel rather than recovering an integer invariant. Importantly, $d_{eff}$ also serves as an a priori structural diagnostic. Driving $d_{eff}$ of a well-posed problem to zero certifies that the physics and boundary constraints have absorbed the network's free directions. Building on this characterization, we introduce subspace projection strategies for boundary adaptation. Rather than retraining from scratch, we project parameter updates into the null space of the pre-trained physics operator so that new boundary conditions are satisfied without disturbing the learned physics. Gradient-based fine-tuning can match or exceed this but needs more wall-clock time and tuning, whereas subspace projection delivers near-equivalent quality in seconds to minutes. We validate on linear and nonlinear operators, demonstrating accurate adaptation to initial and boundary shifts and unencountered constraint types.

</details>


### [126] [Diffusion Models Observe Only Gradients: A Geometric Perspective on Score Matching Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06179)
*Naïl B. Khelifa,Richard E. Turner,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明L²分数匹配误差并非衡量扩散模型边缘分布质量的正确指标，只有梯度分量（而非螺线管分量）影响边际Fokker-Planck动力学。作者给出不可能性定理、更紧的KL散度上界以及可计算的梯度分量估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的理论分析以L²分数匹配误差为核心，但实践中该指标与样本质量关联不佳。作者旨在揭示其几何根源并修正理论框架。

Method: 利用Helmholtz-Hodge分解将分数误差拆解为梯度分量和螺线管分量，分别分析两者对边际分布动力学的影响；通过Girsanov定理和Sobolev对偶推导理论边界与估计器。

Result: (1) 不可能性：L²分数误差的任意单调函数无法一致下界化分布散度；(2) 更紧KL上界仅依赖梯度分量，原Girsanov界的松驰源于路径空间而非边际空间视角；(3) 提出梯度分量的可计算估计器，与样本质量的相关性显著优于完整L²误差。

Conclusion: L²分数误差并非衡量分布匹配的正确指标，应关注梯度分量；理论分析与实践评估需转向边际空间视角。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models are typically trained by minimizing the $L^2$ score matching error, and standard theoretical analyses rely on this quantity to bound the sampling discrepancy between the learned and target distributions. We show the $L^2$ score error is not the right intrinsic measure of marginal distributional quality: a learned diffusion model can incur arbitrarily large $L^2$ score error while perfectly matching the target distribution. By decomposing score errors into a gradient and a solenoidal component (a Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition), we identify the geometric reason behind this: only the gradient component enters the marginal Fokker-Planck dynamics, while the solenoidal component is structurally invisible. We make this precise in three results. First, building on the corrected geometry, we prove an impossibility result: no monotone function of the $L^2$ score error can uniformly lower bound any divergence between the learned and target distributions. Second, we derive an upper bound on the Kullback-Leibler divergence that depends only on the observable gradient component of the error, tightening the standard Girsanov bound and identifying its looseness as the cost of operating on path-space rather than marginal-space dynamics. Third, we give a tractable estimator of the gradient component via a dual Sobolev identity, which is shown to empirically correlate substantially better with sample quality than the full $L^2$ error.

</details>


### [127] [Anchor PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06233)
*Benedikt Seiter,Anya Fries,Julius von Kügelgen,Jonas Peters*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Anchor PCA方法，用于多领域数据的鲁棒无监督降维。该方法通过平衡整体方差解释与领域间共享低秩嵌入的一致性，在修改后的目标矩阵上执行PCA，高效地恢复最大不变子空间，并在模拟和真实气体传感器数据上证明其对未见领域具有更好的方差解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PCA对多领域数据进行池化后降维时，容易聚焦于仅在少数领域呈现高方差的虚假方向，导致嵌入缺乏鲁棒性。本文旨在找到一种能解释未见相似领域中大部分方差、且在不同领域间保持一致的共享低秩嵌入。

Method: 提出Anchor PCA方法：通过构建一个修改后的目标矩阵（在整体方差解释与共享嵌入和领域特定嵌入之间的一致性之间进行权衡），在该矩阵上执行标准PCA即可高效求解。该方法可恢复最大不变子空间，并具有领域特定协方差膨胀下的极小极大重构解释。

Result: 在模拟数据上，Anchor PCA能够恢复最大不变子空间；在具有时间漂移的真实气体传感器数据上，Anchor PCA得到的嵌入在未见领域上比池化PCA基线方法和最坏情况替代方法解释了更多方差。

Conclusion: Anchor PCA是一种有前景的鲁棒无监督降维方法，特别适用于多领域数据场景，能够在保证计算效率的同时提升对未见领域的泛化能力。

Abstract: Principal component analysis (PCA) is one of the most widely used unsupervised dimension reduction techniques. We study PCA for data from multiple related domains. Since principal components generally differ across domains, one way to obtain a shared low-rank embedding is to perform PCA on the pooled data. However, this approach can focus on spurious directions that exhibit high variation in only a few domains. To find a robust embedding that still explains most variance in unseen but similar domains, we propose instead to focus on shared directions of variation. To this end, we introduce Anchor PCA which trades off overall explained variance with agreement between the shared and domain-specific low-rank embeddings. Anchor PCA amounts to PCA on a modified target matrix and thus can be solved efficiently. Moreover, we show that Anchor PCA recovers a maximal invariant subspace and admits a minimax reconstruction interpretation under bounded domain-specific covariance inflations. On simulated and real-world gas sensor data with temporal drift, we demonstrate, respectively, that Anchor PCA recovers the maximally invariant subspace and yields embeddings that explain more variance on unseen domains than the pooling baseline and a worst-case alternative. Taken together, these findings establish Anchor PCA as a promising approach to robust unsupervised dimension reduction from multi-domain data.

</details>


### [128] [Discrete Causal Representations from Heterogeneous Domains: A Bayesian Approach with Social Survey Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06288)
*Ankur Garg,Michael Stettler,Aaron Schein,Julius von Kügelgen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯方法，用于从多环境数据中学习离散潜在因果概念的因果表示，通过序列蒙特卡洛采样近似多模态后验，并在社会调查数据中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管因果表示的可识别性已被广泛研究，但实用化的不确定性感知方法和真实世界用例仍较少被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出一种能够在多环境数据中学习因果表示并量化不确定性的贝叶斯方法。

Method: 将因果假设和可解释性需求转化为层级模型中的适当先验和参数选择；针对离散因果概念和未知多节点软干预，设计基于序列蒙特卡洛采样的推理方案来近似多模态后验。

Result: 在社会调查数据（潜在因果概念对应文化价值观或政治观点，测量对应调查响应，环境对应不同国家或州）上的案例研究表明，模型能够推断出有意义的潜在概念及它们之间合理的因果关系。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯方法能够有效学习复杂真实世界数据的因果表示，推断出有意义的高层概念和因果结构，展示了在跨环境数据中应用因果表示学习的潜力。

Abstract: Causal representation learning aims to infer the high-level latent causal concepts that give rise to observed low-level measurements. This is particularly relevant for heterogeneous data from different environments or domains since distribution shifts often arise through sparse, localized changes in some of the underlying causal mechanisms, while other parts of the generative process remain unchanged. Whereas identifiability of causal representations has been studied extensively, practical uncertainty-aware methods and real-world use cases remain less explored. In this work, we propose a Bayesian approach to learning causal representations from multi-environment data, focusing on the case of discrete causal concepts and unknown multi-node soft interventions. To this end, we translate causal assumptions and interpretability desiderata into suitable priors and parametric choices within a hierarchical model. We then devise an inference scheme based on sequential Monte Carlo sampling to approximate the resulting multimodal posterior. We showcase our approach through case studies on social survey data, where latent causal concepts correspond to cultural values or political opinions, measurements to survey responses, and environments to different countries or states. Our model infers meaningful high-level concepts and plausible causal relations among them, demonstrating its utility for learning causal representations of complex real-world data.

</details>


### [129] [Symmetric Divergence and Normalized Similarity: A Unified Topological Framework for Representation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06342)
*Yan Wang,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的拓扑数据分析工具包，包括对称表示拓扑散度（SRTD/SRTD-lite）和归一化拓扑相似度（NTS），分别用于细粒度结构诊断和标准化跨场景基准评估，解决了现有拓扑散度的非对称性和样本依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成对拓扑散度（如RTD）存在非对称性以及分数随样本量变化而无界的问题，阻碍了可靠的跨场景基准评估。需要一种既能进行细粒度结构诊断，又能进行标准化评估的统一拓扑工具。

Method: 1) 提出对称表示拓扑散度（SRTD）及其高效变体SRTD-lite，通过完整跨条形码签名解决非对称性问题，无需双向计算。2) 提出归一化拓扑相似度（NTS），通过测量层次合并顺序的秩相关，得到-1到1之间的尺度不变度量，克服未归一化散度的样本依赖问题。

Result: 在合成数据和真实深度学习场景中，该工具包能够捕捉几何度量无法发现的CNN功能变化，并在距离饱和条件下稳健地映射LLM谱系。

Conclusion: 该工具包提供了严格的拓扑感知视角，能够与CKA等度量互补，在细粒度诊断和标准化评估两个维度上均有显著优势。

Abstract: Topological Data Analysis (TDA) offers a principled, intrinsic lens for comparing neural representations. However, existing paired topological divergences (e.g., RTD) are limited by heuristic asymmetry and, more critically, unbounded scores that depend on sample size, hindering reliable cross-scenario benchmarking. To address these challenges, we develop a unified topological toolkit serving two complementary needs: fine-grained structural diagnosis and robust, standardized evaluation. First, we complete the RTD framework by introducing Symmetric Representation Topology Divergence (SRTD) and its efficient variant SRTD-lite. Beyond resolving the theoretical asymmetry of prior variants, SRTD consolidates diagnostic information into a single, comprehensive cross-barcode signature. This allows for precise localization of structural discrepancies and serves as an effective optimization objective without the overhead of dual directional computations. Second, to enable reliable benchmarking across heterogeneous settings, we propose Normalized Topological Similarity (NTS). By measuring the rank correlation of hierarchical merge orders, NTS yields a scale-invariant metric bounded between -1 and 1, effectively overcoming the scale and sample-dependence of unnormalized divergences. Experiments across synthetic and real-world deep learning settings demonstrate that our toolkit captures functional shifts in CNNs missed by geometric measures and robustly maps LLM genealogy even under distance saturation, offering a rigorous, topology-aware perspective that complements measures like CKA.

</details>


### [130] [Function-Space Priors for Bayesian Neural ODEs with Application to Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06351)
*Jaeyeong Lee,Wonmo Koo,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合函数空间正则化和概率多重打靶的贝叶斯神经常微分方程方法，用于处理AIS数据中不规则采样和缺失报告的船舶轨迹预测与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯神经常微分方程使用各向同性高斯权重先验，无法编码船舶动力学中的平滑性和局部性等结构特性；而函数空间贝叶斯神经网络方法虽能解决此问题，但无法直接迁移到神经常微分方程场景中。

Method: 在有限测量点上的向量场施加基于高斯过程核的先验，将核正则化项引入权重空间变分目标中；并进一步结合概率多重打靶方法，将推理分解到时间片段上，同时保持全局一致性。

Result: 该方法能够处理长序列和不规则采样的AIS轨迹数据，同时提供校准良好的不确定性估计，适用于需要可靠决策支持的海事应用场景。

Conclusion: 通过函数空间正则化与概率多重打靶的融合，有效提升了贝叶斯神经常微分方程在船舶轨迹预测任务中的建模能力，兼顾了结构先验引入与长时序列计算可扩展性。

Abstract: Vessel trajectory prediction from Automatic Identification System (AIS) data is essential for maritime situational awareness, yet it remains challenging due to irregular sampling, missing reports, and complex dynamics. Beyond accurate point forecasts, maritime applications also demand well-calibrated uncertainty estimates for reliable decision-making. Bayesian Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) offer a principled framework for continuous-time trajectory modeling with uncertainty quantification by placing a prior over the neural vector field parameters. However, the commonly used isotropic Gaussian weight prior fails to encode informative structural properties of vessel dynamics, such as smoothness and locality. Existing function-space Bayesian neural network methods address this limitation for static mappings, but do not transfer directly to Neural ODEs, where the primary quantity of interest is the trajectory rather than the vector field itself. In principle, one could place a Gaussian process (GP) prior directly over ODE solutions, but this requires propagating distributions through a nonlinear ODE solver, which is analytically intractable. To address this challenge, we adopt a practical approach that imposes a GP-kernel-based prior directly on the vector field evaluated at a finite set of measurement points. Specifically, we augment the standard weight-space variational objective with a kernel-based regularizer that penalizes deviations of the vector field from the structure implied by a GP prior. To handle long and irregular AIS trajectories, we further combine this function-space regularization with probabilistic multiple shooting, which decouples inference across temporal segments while maintaining global consistency.

</details>


### [131] [Conformal Risk Sharing: Certified Cost Allocation with Participation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06391)
*Ieva Kazlauskaite*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Risk Sharing框架，通过可解释的分担策略与分裂共形校准，从有限数据中生成无分布假设的认证分配方案，为每个参与者提供可信的上限承诺，并验证无人受损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在群体共担罕见负面事件财务影响时，任何因分配而变差的参与者都有理由退出，因此需要一种可信机制，为每个主体提供未来义务的上限承诺，并确保群体总损失有界，从而激励参与并维持合作稳定性。

Method: 将问题形式化为认证分配问题，提出Conformal Risk Sharing方法：结合可解释的分担策略与分裂共形校准（split conformal calibration），在训练数据上调整分担强度，在保留的校准数据上生成分布自由（仅需可交换性）的逐主体保证。

Result: 在合成数据以及真实降水、能源合作社数据上的实验表明，该方法能显著降低高风险主体的极端义务，同时控制对其他主体的伤害。

Conclusion: Conformal Risk Sharing为无分布假设下的风险共担提供了可验证、个体理性的认证分配框架，在主体异质性场景中有效平衡了集体分担与个体保护。

Abstract: Sharing the financial impact of rare adverse events across a group can soften extreme individual burdens, but any participant made worse off by the arrangement has reason to leave. A credible mechanism must therefore provide each agent with a trustworthy cap on their future obligation and should be deployed only if the aggregate harm across participants is bounded. We formalise this as the Certified Allocation Problem: from finite data and without distributional assumptions, find a redistribution rule, produce obligation caps for every participant, and verify that no participant is made materially worse off. We propose Conformal Risk Sharing, which solves this problem by pairing an interpretable sharing policy with split conformal calibration. The sharing intensity is tuned on training data, while held-out calibration data produces distribution-free per-agent guarantees (valid under exchangeability). Experiments on synthetic and real-world data, including precipitation and energy-cooperative data, confirm that the framework can substantially reduce extreme obligations for high-risk agents while controlling harm to others.

</details>


### [132] [Central Description Length (CDL) Clustering Validation Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05230)
*Mahdi Shamsi,Soosan Beheshti*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出中心描述长度（CDL）聚类验证指标，利用簇内紧致度、估计聚类中心和协方差计算真实聚类中心描述长度的概率上界，无需标签即可评估聚类质量。在非凸、任意形状数据集上比传统CVI更准确选择聚类数，在图像基准测试中也表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的聚类验证指标（CVI）多基于欧氏紧致度和分离度构建，倾向于选择紧凑、凸形划分，在非凸、不规则或密度变化的数据上性能退化，且通常需要额外的核变换或距离度量调整，增加计算开销。

Method: CDL利用观测到的簇内紧致度、估计的聚类中心和协方差，计算不可观测的真实聚类中心描述长度的概率上界，将簇内紧致度和中心位移压缩为单一可计算量，仅需数据、划分结果、估计中心和协方差，无需真实标签。

Result: 在非凸和任意形状合成基准上，CDL-CVI比传统CVI更频繁地选择参考聚类数并达到更高的调整兰德指数（ARI）；在MNIST、CIFAR-10、STL-10图像基准上，针对K-means、DBSCAN和谱聚类，CDL-CVI返回的聚类数接近参考类别数。

Conclusion: CDL-CVI无需额外核预处理即可有效评估非凸、不规则形状数据的聚类质量，在多种聚类算法和图像基准上均表现良好。

Abstract: Selecting a clustering algorithm and its hyperparameters without labels is a common difficulty in engineering machine learning pipelines that work with unsupervised analysis of sensor, image, or process data. Clustering validation indices (CVIs) provide internal scores for ranking candidate clusterings, but most popular CVIs are built from Euclidean compactness and separation terms and so tend to favour compact, convex partitions. Their performance is known to degrade on non convex, irregular, or variable density data, where kernel transformations or alternative distance measures are typically used at the cost of additional tuning and computation. This paper introduces the Central Description Length (CDL) clustering validation index. CDL uses the observed within cluster compactness, the estimated cluster centers, and the estimated cluster covariances to compute a probabilistic upper bound on the description length associated with the unobservable true cluster centers. The bound condenses intra cluster compactness and centroid displacement into a single computable quantity and is evaluated on the partition produced by any clustering algorithm. The implementation uses only observable quantities (the data, the partition, the estimated centers, and the estimated covariances) and does not use ground truth labels. On synthetic benchmarks with non convex and arbitrary shape clusters, CDL-CVI selected the reference number of clusters more often and reached higher Adjusted Rand Index (ARI) values than the conventional CVIs we tested, without an additional kernel preprocessing stage. On image benchmarks (MNIST, CIFAR-10, STL-10) clustered from frozen unsupervised embeddings, CDL-CVI returned cluster numbers close to the reference class counts across K-means, DBSCAN, and spectral clustering in the reported trials.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [The Evaluation Blind Spot: A Stereological Theory of Benchmark Coverage for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05169)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM基准测试覆盖的体视学理论，证明了基准套件的有效维度d_eff决定了可见能力轮廓之间的Hausdorff距离界。实验发现三个独立排行榜的d_eff在[2.86, 4.80]之间，结构盲点比观察到的亚军得分差距大两个数量级。通过子模贪心算法找到4个基准的稳定核心，12个中的7个即可达到90%覆盖率。此外，独立解决了Gardner问题1.5 (1995)关于C^2支撑函数的极小极大率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准评估存在覆盖盲区，需要一种理论框架来量化基准套件对模型能力的覆盖程度，并识别哪些基准是冗余的、哪些是不可替代的。

Method: 1) 建立体视学理论框架，定义有效维度d_eff并推导Hausdorff距离界；2) 对三个独立排行榜进行实证分析，计算有效维度和结构盲点；3) 使用卡方投影模型进行模拟验证；4) 采用子模贪心算法（Nemhauser (1-1/e)保证）寻找最优基准子集；5) 对12个内部基准和27个Chatbot Arena类别进行反事实验证；6) 独立解决Gardner问题1.5，通过S^(D-1)上的最优恢复理论建立极小极大率。

Result: 1) 三个排行榜的有效维度在[2.86, 4.80]之间；2) 结构盲点比亚军差距大两个数量级，比统计噪声大52-127倍；3) 在多种隐含能力先验下，top-2模型半分割交换率保持在[0.38, 0.49]；4) 500次随机可见/保留分割中92%交换了top-1排名，平均2.83/5个top-5模型变化；5) 4个基准构成稳定核心，7/12即可达90%覆盖率，跨季度迁移保持93-97%；6) 特征结构预测哪些评估不可替代（移除扰动rho=-0.69, p=0.013）和哪些外部评估带来新信息（rho=+0.38）；7) Gardner问题1.5的解为Theta(R/(kappa m^(2/(D-1))))。

Conclusion: LLM基准套件存在显著的结构性盲点，当前排行榜排名对基准选择高度敏感；通过有效维度分析可以系统性地识别冗余和不可替代的基准，为构建高效评估套件提供理论指导；Gardner问题的解决为凸几何中的支撑函数逼近提供了理论下限。

Abstract: We give a stereological theory of LLM benchmark coverage. For any suite with effective dimensionality d_eff, the visible Hausdorff distance between two convex capability profiles consistent with the same scores is bounded by epsilon + C R m^(-1/(d_eff-1)), with matching Lipschitz lower bound. Empirically, three independent leaderboards (Open LLM v2, an extended 12-benchmark suite, LiveBench) all have d_eff in [2.86, 4.80] on their competitive frontier; the structural blind spot exceeds the observed runner-up score gap by two orders of magnitude and dominates statistical noise by 52-127x. Under a chi-squared projection model, the isotropic prior is the optimistic case; across six hidden-capability priors and four ambient dimensions the simulated half-split swap rate of the top two models stays in [0.38, 0.49], and a 500-trial random visible/held-out split shows that 92% of trials swap the top-1 ranking with on average 2.83 of 5 top-5 models changing. A submodular greedy algorithm with the Nemhauser (1 - 1/e) guarantee finds a stable core of 4 benchmarks; 7 of 12 suffice for 90% coverage, and the trained subset transfers across temporal quarters with 93-97% retention. A counterfactual validation across 12 internal benchmarks and 27 Chatbot Arena categories confirms that the eigenstructure predicts which evaluations are irreplaceable (rho = -0.69, p = 0.013 for removal disruption) and which external evaluations bring new information (rho = +0.38). As a second, independent theoretical contribution, we resolve Gardner's Problem 1.5 (1995) for C^2 support functions, establishing the minimax rate Theta(R/(kappa m^(2/(D-1)))) in general dimension via optimal recovery theory on S^(D-1).

</details>


### [134] [ERRORQUAKE: Heavy-Tailed Error Severity Distributions in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05170)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Errorquake-10k基准，通过连续0-4级严重度评分评估21个开源LLM，发现模型在相同准确率下错误严重度分布存在显著差异，证明严重度分布与错误率是信息非冗余的，应作为独立指标报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉评测只报告单一错误率，将所有错误等同对待，但"错误日期"与"虚构法庭裁决"的危害程度相差数个数量级，因此需要能捕捉错误严重度分布的评测方法。

Method: 构建Errorquake-10k基准（1万查询，8领域×5难度），采用连续0-4级严重度评分；为21个开源模型拟合Gutenberg-Richter严重度分布指数b；通过519项三人人工验证评测可靠性（ICC=0.85）和LLM裁判排序效度（ρ=0.89）；证明非冗余性定理。

Result: 210个模型对中85个在匹配准确率下b的95%置信区间不重叠；非冗余性定理表明严重度分布与错误率信息非冗余（互信息1.56比特，64.5%跨模型b方差无法由ε解释）；错误类型随严重度呈范畴性转变（低严重度71%为检索错误，高严重度39%为捏造）。

Conclusion: 严重度分布应作为准确率的补充指标报告，因为它携带错误率无法提供的判别信息。

Abstract: At matched accuracy, open-weight LLMs differ substantially in the shape of their error severity distribution -- a difference invisible to the scalar error rate. Hallucination benchmarks report a single error count and treat all errors as equivalent, yet a wrong date and a fabricated court ruling differ by orders of magnitude. We introduce Errorquake-10k, a 10,000-query benchmark scoring each response on a continuous 0-4 severity scale across 8 domains and 5 difficulty tiers, and we fit per-model severity distributions for 21 open-weight models. For each model we estimate a severity distribution index (b, the Gutenberg-Richter upper-tail slope) with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals. Headline: across the 210 model pairs, 85 have disjoint 95% b confidence intervals at matched accuracy (|Delta epsilon| < 0.05) on human-consensus scoring, e.g. deepseek-v3.2 vs. ministral-14b at epsilon = 0.586 and Delta b = 0.47. A 519-item three-rater human validation study confirms measurement reliability (ICC(2,k=3) = 0.85), validates the LLM-judge ranking (rho = 0.89), and confirms the dense-model scaling correlation on human data (rho_s = -0.86). We prove a Non-Reducibility Theorem showing that severity profile and error rate are informationally non-redundant (I(b; model | epsilon) = 1.56 bits; 64.5% of cross-model b variance is unexplained by epsilon). A severity mechanism taxonomy (kappa = 0.83) reveals that error type shifts categorically with severity: low-severity errors are retrievals (71%); high-severity errors are fabrications (39%) -- and this composition differs by model size (p < 0.0001). Severity distribution should be reported alongside accuracy; it carries discriminative information that the error rate cannot.

</details>


### [135] [Staged Factorial Screening for Budget-Constrained Micro-Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05186)
*Felipe Chavarro Polania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在预算约束下的微预训练中，如何通过分阶段分数阶乘工作流来恢复稳定的早期效应结构。通过613次实验和多种基线比较，发现总批量、深度和宽度的主惩罚在短预算下最大，随着预算增加而放松。最终提出了一种有界方法：使用短设计筛选来识别高惩罚方向，确认有希望的锚点，并在缩减空间内局部优化。证据支持以桥接为中心的建议，但并非硬件不变的排名或通用超参数优化优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预算约束的微预训练中，需要在共享加速器上对大量候选配方进行筛选，然后才能投入更大的搜索预算。目前缺乏系统的方法来恢复稳定的早期效应结构，导致资源浪费和效率低下。

Method: 采用分阶段分数阶乘工作流，在固定自研单GPU训练循环上运行613次实验，包括预实验和后续筛选（2、5、10分钟）、完整16条件播种重跑、播种锚点检查、同主机贪婪和匹配成本随机基线、60分钟桥接包，以及24小时内的A100和L40S锚点延续。

Result: 主惩罚（总批量、深度、宽度）在短预算下最大，随预算增加而放松。在预先声明的播种全筛选族中，D、A、B、C在5和10分钟后仍保持非零估计，而E则不显著。随机搜索可达到强候选解，但重复在低惩罚区域且无法进行因子归因。60分钟桥接锚点均值最低，但无法分离工作流改进与桥接模型容量优势。在12小时和24小时的三锚点延续中，桥接锚点样本均值最低，非桥接排序保持主机敏感性。

Conclusion: 提出有界方法：使用短设计筛选识别高惩罚方向，在重复运行下确认有希望的锚点，并在缩减空间内局部优化。证据支持桥接中心建议（24小时内两个主机），但并非硬件不变排名或通用超参数优化优越性。

Abstract: Budget-constrained micro-pretraining often requires triaging many candidate recipes on a shared accelerator before larger search budgets are spent. We study whether a staged fractional-factorial workflow can recover stable early effect structure in this setting. On a fixed autoresearch-derived single-GPU training loop, we run 613 experiments across pilot and follow-up screens at 2, 5, and 10 minutes; full 16-condition seeded reruns at 5 and 10 minutes; targeted seeded anchor checks; same-host greedy and matched-cost random baselines; a 60-minute bridge package; and bounded Windows A100 and Linux L40S anchor continuations through 24 hours. Main penalties from total batch, depth, and width are largest at short budgets and relax as budget increases. Within the predeclared seeded full-screen families, D, A, B, and C retain non-zero estimates at 5 and 10 minutes after within-budget Benjamini-Hochberg correction, while E does not. Random search can reach strong incumbents in this 32-condition space, but repeatedly in the same low-penalty region and without factor attribution. The 60-minute bridge anchor has the lowest mean, although that package does not separate workflow refinement from the larger bridge model's capacity advantage. In bounded 12-hour and 24-hour three-anchor continuations on both hosts, the bridge has the lowest sample mean while the non-bridge ordering stays host-sensitive. We therefore present a bounded methods result: use short designed screens to identify high-penalty directions, confirm promising anchors under repeated runs, and refine locally inside the reduced space. The evidence supports a bridge-centered recommendation through 24 hours on two hosts, not hardware-invariant ranking or general hyperparameter-optimization superiority.

</details>


### [136] [PyCC.id: A package for hypothesis-driven equation discovery with structural identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05191)
*Federico J. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PyCC库，一种基于特征曲线(CC)骨架的结构化方法，用于从时间序列数据中发现ODE方程，通过结合假设和先验知识来缓解逆问题的不适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动方程发现是一个不适定的逆问题，常产生多个拟合效果相似的候选模型。传统方法依赖领域专家进行事后手动筛选，缺乏系统性。

Method: 提出基于特征曲线(CC)的骨架方法：用户定义与ODE族关联的骨架结构，结合领域假设和先验知识，迭代精化模型。该方法具有结构可辨识性，支持神经网络、符号回归、稀疏回归等多种范式。

Result: 开发了Python库PyCC，将上述方法封装为灵活工具，支持研究人员和工程师无缝定义骨架和假设以从时间依赖数据中发现ODE。

Conclusion: PyCC通过结构化骨架假设方法，有效缩小解空间并提供可辨识性检查，为时间序列数据中的ODE发现提供了模块化、假设驱动的实用工具。

Abstract: Data-driven equation discovery is fundamentally an inverse problem that seeks to infer the governing differential equations of a system directly from time-series measurements. A known issue is the ill-conditioned nature of the inverse problem, which frequently produces multiple mathematical models that fit the data similarly well. One path to address this issue is by incorporating known hypotheses and constraints into the training phase beforehand. While this approach effectively reduces the search space, it still results in multiple candidate models, forcing practitioners to rely on post-hoc manual filtering based on their own domain expertise. A recent approach incorporates structural `skeletons' inspired by characteristic curves (CCs), defining a hypothesis-driven methodology. In this methodology, practitioners define a skeleton, which is associated with a family of ordinary differential equations (ODEs), and then add their hypotheses and priors based on their domain knowledge to refine the obtained model iteratively. An important advantage of this approach is that some skeletons have demonstrable structural identifiability properties, which are useful for checking whether the skeleton is correct or should be discarded. Furthermore, this formalism enables the use of multiple equation discovery paradigms due to its modularity (such as neural networks, symbolic regression, and sparse regression). In this work, we present the Python library PyCC, which condenses these efforts into a flexible tool that allows researchers and engineers to seamlessly define their skeletons and hypotheses to discover ODEs from time-dependent data.

</details>


### [137] [Temporal Preference Concepts and their Functions in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05194)
*Ian Rios-Sialer,Shantanu Darveshi,Shuai Jiang,Avigya Paudel,Anastasiia Pronina,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过机制可解释性方法，在蒸馏LLM中定位了控制时间偏好（当前收益 vs 长期后果权衡）的因果子图，发现模型对未来折扣率远低于人类且不稳定，并初步证明通过引导向量可调控时间偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs越来越多地被部署用于需要权衡短期收益与长期后果的决策场景，但人们对其内部如何表征和解决这种权衡知之甚少，因此需要理解其时间偏好的内部机制以实现可靠控制。

Method: 在蒸馏LLM（Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507）上，通过梯度归因和激活补丁的汇聚证据因果定位时间偏好的底层子图（中上层节点）；分析残差流中时间跨度的几何编码；通过行为实验比较人类与LLM的折扣率；使用引导向量尝试调控时间偏好。

Result: 时间偏好的因果子图位于模型的中上层；残差流在预期的局部层编码了时间跨度的几何结构；未经干预的LLM对未来折扣率远低于人类（更偏好长期），但该偏好不稳定；引导向量可初步证明能转移时间偏好。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性有助于实现对LLM规划与推理中时间偏好的可靠控制，应主动干预而非隐式依赖训练。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed to make decisions that require trading off near-term gains against long-term consequences, yet little is known about how they internally represent or resolve these tradeoffs. In this work, we causally localize an underlying subgraph for temporal preference in a distilled LLM (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), identifying mid-to-upper-layer nodes through converging evidence from gradient-based attribution and activation patching. We find that the geometry of time horizon is encoded in the residual stream at the expected localized layers. A behavioral analysis reveals that unintervened LLMs discount the future several times less steeply than humans, yet this preference is unstable across contexts, motivating explicit control rather than implicit reliance on training. Finally, we find suggestive evidence that steering vectors can shift temporal preference. Our work demonstrates how mechanistic interpretability can bring us closer to reliable control over how LLMs plan and reason

</details>


### [138] [State commitment learning: training language models to distinguish computation from memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05201)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出状态承诺学习（State Commitment Learning）及其实现方法CERL（反事实擦除强化学习），训练推理语言模型区分持久状态与临时计算，使模型在擦除隐藏思考后仍能保持答案正确性，在数学、逻辑推理、科学问答和多轮工具使用等任务上优于传统RL和SFT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理语言模型不区分用于计算的token和用于持久状态的token，所有隐藏思考（包括失败尝试、死胡同和私密草稿）都保留在上下文中，影响后续预测，导致下游推理可能依赖不应被安全依赖的内容。

Method: 提出状态承诺学习训练目标，定义持久状态充分性（Persistent-State Sufficiency）作为反事实准则。提出反事实擦除强化学习（CERL），在相同前缀下比较保留隐藏思考路径与擦除隐藏思考路径，仅当擦除路径仍正确时给予奖励。同时引入擦除依赖协议（Erasure Dependence Protocol）进行评估。

Result: 在数学、长链逻辑、科学QA和多轮工具使用评估中，CERL显著降低答案对隐藏思考的依赖，同时不牺牲准确性，一致优于仅正确性RL和长答案SFT基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式训练模型区分持久状态与临时计算，可以有效减少推理过程中对隐藏思考的不必要依赖，提升推理的鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Reasoning language models do not distinguish tokens used for computation from tokens that constitute persistent state: once generated, all hidden thoughts remain in context and influence future predictions. As a result, downstream reasoning may depend on failed attempts, dead ends, and private scratch work that should not be safely relied on later. We recast this phenomenon as a new training objective, state commitment learning: training models to explicitly distinguish information that should be committed as persistent state from temporary computation that can be discarded. We define a counterfactual criterion, persistent-state sufficiency, which makes it trainable and measurable whether an answer remains usable after hidden thoughts are erased. We then propose Counterfactual Erasure RL (CERL), which evaluates, under the same prefix, both a path that keeps hidden thoughts and a path that erases them, and gives reward only when the erasure path remains correct. We also introduce the Erasure Dependence Protocol and show across mathematics, long-chain logic, scientific QA, and multi-turn tool-use evaluation that CERL substantially reduces answer dependence on hidden thoughts without sacrificing accuracy, consistently outperforming correctness-only RL and long-answer SFT baselines.

</details>


### [139] [Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Restores Symmetry in Deep Linear Networks with Multi-Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05219)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度线性网络中梯度下降（GD）在较大步长下的路径竞争行为，发现大步长GD会导致信号在多个路径间重新分配，而非梯度流（GF）预测的“赢家通吃”式单路径特化。原因是单路径解是尖锐极小值，而多路径分布能降低尖锐度，步长越大、深度越深时这种效应越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示离散梯度下降（GD）在大步长下与梯度流（GF）预测不同的路径竞争机制，解释为何大步长GD倾向于共享表示而非单路径主导。

Method: 通过理论证明单路径解为尖锐极小值，多路径分布可降低尖锐度；结合早期训练（对称性破缺）与边缘稳定性（Edge of Stability）阶段的振荡分析，描述信号再平衡过程。

Result: 大步长GD的早期阶段复现GF预测的深度驱动对称性破缺，但随后边缘稳定性阶段的振荡会覆盖这一趋势，驱动信号在路径间重新分配。

Conclusion: 深度影响路径竞争的方式：深度越大，多路径分布降低尖锐度的因子越大；大步长GD有利于共享表示，而非持续的单路径主导。

Abstract: Recent analyses of multi-pathway Deep Linear Networks use Gradient Flow to predict a "winner-takes-all" specialization in which path symmetry breaks and each feature concentrates in a single pathway. In this work, we show that discrete Gradient Descent (GD) with a large step size tells a different story. We prove that single-path solutions are sharp minima, whereas distributing signals across pathways reduces sharpness by a factor that decreases with both the number of pathways and depth. Consequently, while early training reproduces the depth-driven symmetry breaking predicted by GF, oscillations at the Edge of Stability subsequently override this tendency and drive the network into a re-balancing phase, where signals redistribute across pathways. Together, these results clarify how depth shapes pathway competition and explain why large-step GD favors shared representations rather than persistent single-pathway dominance.

</details>


### [140] [Differentiable Efficient Operator Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05232)
*Xiaohuan Pei,Jiyuan Zhang,Yuanfan Guo,Weiguo Feng,Tao Huang,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高效算子搜索（Efficient Operator Search）框架，将多种手动设计的token缩减算子统一到共享算子空间中，并通过可微分搜索自动优化层激活、保留预算和算子行为，在多模态基准测试中实现竞争性的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态基础模型依赖手动设计的token缩减算子（剪枝、合并、池化、自适应重加权等），这些算子虽然看似不同，但缺乏统一的视角和自动化设计方法，限制了效率提升的潜力。

Method: 提出可微分搜索框架Efficient Operator Search，将token缩减算子统一到共享算子空间，联合搜索三个维度：哪里缩减（层激活）、保留多少token（保留预算）以及如何处理缩减后的信息（算子行为）。搜索策略在单边预算和成本约束下优化任务性能。

Result: 该公式能够恢复代表性手动设计基线作为特例，并发现超越孤立手动设计的混合算子。多模态基准实验表明，搜索得到的算子在精度-效率权衡上具有竞争力，尤其在激进的视觉token缩减场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 高效多模态推理可以从手动算子设计转向可微分算子搜索，为自动化设计高效多模态基础模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Efficient multimodal foundation models often rely on manually designed token-reduction operators, such as pruning, merging, pooling, and adaptive reweighting. Although these operators appear different, we show that they can be interpreted as distinct regimes of a shared operator space. Based on this view, we introduce Efficient Operator Search, a differentiable framework that jointly searches where to reduce tokens, how many tokens to retain, and how reduced token information should be processed. The proposed search space parameterizes layer activation, retention budget, and operator behavior, while the search policy optimizes task performance under one-sided budget and cost constraints. This formulation recovers representative hand-designed baselines as special cases and further discovers hybrid operators beyond isolated manual designs. Experiments on multimodal benchmarks show that the searched operators achieve competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs, especially under aggressive visual-token reduction. These results suggest that efficient multimodal inference can be reframed from manual operator design to differentiable operator search.

</details>


### [141] [Sharp First-Order Lower Bounds for Higher-Order Smooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05438)
*Dongruo Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在光滑非凸优化中，当目标函数满足高阶光滑性（如Lipschitz Hessian或三阶导数Lipschitz）时，确定性一阶黑盒算法达到ε-稳定点的最优下界，分别匹配了已知的ε^{-7/4}和ε^{-5/3}上界，填补了理论空白。构造基于一种“块链”（block-chain）机制，并通过ChatGPT 5.5 Pro辅助发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典非凸优化中，仅Lipschitz梯度条件下ε^{-2}的下界是紧的；但当目标函数具有更高阶光滑性（如Lipschitz Hessian或三阶导数）时，已知存在加速的一阶算法上界，但匹配的下界一直缺失。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，证明在高阶光滑条件下这些加速上界的最优性。

Method: 提出一种基于“块链”（block-chain）机制的硬实例构造方法：通过链式结构强制黑盒算法按块逐步揭示信息，同时保持所需的高阶光滑性（如Lipschitz Hessian或三阶导数），从而推导出与已知上界匹配的维数无关的下界。该构造借助ChatGPT 5.5 Pro辅助发现，后经作者验证。

Result: 对于Lipschitz Hessian（二阶光滑）情形，给出匹配的Ω(ε^{-7/4})下界；对于三阶导数Lipschitz（三阶光滑）情形，给出匹配的Ω(ε^{-5/3})下界。下界对任意有限光滑阶均成立，且与维数无关。

Conclusion: 首次证明了高阶光滑非凸优化中一阶算法的最优下界，确认了已知加速上界（ε^{-7/4}和ε^{-5/3}）的不可改进性，为非凸优化中高阶光滑性带来的加速效应提供了完整的理论刻画。

Abstract: We study the deterministic first-order oracle complexity of finding \(ε\)-stationary points in smooth nonconvex optimization when the objective satisfies higher-order smoothness assumptions. While the classical \(ε^{-2}\) rate is optimal under only Lipschitz gradients, higher-order smoothness leads to accelerated first-order upper bounds, most notably the \(ε^{-7/4}\) rate under Lipschitz Hessians and the \(ε^{-5/3}\) rate under Lipschitz third derivatives. The matching lower bounds, however, have remained open. We resolve this gap by proving a new dimension-free first-order lower bound for higher-order smooth nonconvex functions, valid for every finite smoothness order. In particular, our construction gives a matching \(Ω(ε^{-7/4})\) lower bound in the Hessian-Lipschitz case and a matching \(Ω(ε^{-5/3})\) lower bound in the third-order-smooth regime. The hard instance is based on a \emph{block-chain} mechanism that enforces blockwise oracle revelation while preserving the smoothness structure needed for the scalar hard instance. The lower-bound construction was discovered with the assistance of ChatGPT 5.5 Pro and subsequently verified by the authors.

</details>


### [142] [DiffSlack: Learning under Nonlinear Inequality Constraints via Learnable Slack Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05247)
*Ziqian Wang,Chenxi Fang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffSlack，一种可微投影层，用于在神经网络中强制执行非线性不等式约束。该方法将不等式重写为带可学习松弛变量的等式，通过阻尼高斯-牛顿投影将原始预测映射到可行域，并采用两阶段课程训练。在车辆路径规划（200个非线性约束）和CARLA/实车实验中，DiffSlack相比基线方法取得了更高的规划成功率和更强的约束满足能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在神经网络中强制执行非线性不等式约束仍然具有挑战性，尤其是当输出受多个耦合约束限制时。现有的硬约束方法要么对约束集施加结构限制，要么在大规模非线性问题上引入大量计算开销。

Method: DiffSlack包含三个核心组件：(1) 将不等式约束转化为带可学习松弛变量的等式约束，松弛变量作为网络增强输出的一部分进行预测，为阻尼高斯-牛顿投影提供数据驱动的热启动；(2) 可微投影层将原始预测映射到增强可行流形上，同时保持端到端可微性；(3) 两阶段课程训练策略稳定训练过程并改进约束满足效果。

Result: 在车辆路径规划任务（含200个来自碰撞避免、曲率限制和路径点间距的非线性不等式约束）上，DiffSlack在可比推理预算下实现了更高的规划成功率和更强的几何约束满足。消融实验表明硬投影层降低了对监督质量的敏感度。CARLA闭环跟踪和实车实验验证了生成轨迹的可执行性。

Conclusion: DiffSlack为在神经网络中嵌入硬不等式约束提供了一种实用且可扩展的方法，适用于工程应用。

Abstract: Enforcing nonlinear inequality constraints in neural networks remains challenging, especially when the output is subject to many coupled constraints. Existing hard constraint methods often impose structural restrictions on the constraint set or introduce substantial computational overhead for large-scale nonlinear problems. Here, we propose DiffSlack, a differentiable projection layer for nonlinear inequality-constrained neural prediction. DiffSlack reformulates inequalities as equalities with learnable slack variables, which are predicted as part of the augmented network output and provide a data-driven warm start for damped Gauss-Newton projection. The projection layer maps raw predictions onto the augmented feasible manifold while preserving end-to-end differentiability. A two-stage curriculum further stabilizes training and improves constraint satisfaction. We evaluate DiffSlack on vehicle path planning with 200 nonlinear inequality constraints from collision avoidance, curvature limits, and waypoint spacing. Compared with existing learning-based baselines, DiffSlack achieves a higher planning success rate and stronger geometric constraint satisfaction under a comparable inference budget. Ablation studies further show that the hard projection layer reduces sensitivity to supervision quality. Closed-loop tracking in CARLA and real-world vehicle experiments confirms the executability of the generated trajectories. These results demonstrate that DiffSlack provides a practical and scalable approach to embedding hard inequality constraints into neural networks for engineering applications.

</details>


### [143] [Alpha-RTL: Test-Time Training for RTL Hardware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05253)
*Peilong Zhou,Zhirong Chen,Cangyuan Li,Haoyu Gao,Kaiyan Chang,Ziming Qu,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TTT-RTL，首个面向RTL设计的测试时训练框架，通过在测试时利用EDA反馈（语法检查、仿真、综合PPA）在线更新LLM策略，在RTLLM v2.0上PPA乘积降低65.1%，优于冻结策略基线（26.1%），并在工业级C910 FPU单元上实现59.4%的ADP降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成RTL设计的方法要么训练通用生成器后直接部署（无法适应特定设计需求），要么使用冻结策略进行测试时搜索（缺乏在线适应能力），无法充分利用EDA反馈信号来优化具体RTL问题的物理性能。

Method: TTT-RTL框架：(1)采样候选实现并通过语法检查和仿真验证；(2)使用综合导出的PPA乘积对有效设计评分；(3)通过PUCT索引的设计状态池重用高奖励变体；(4)使用熵策略梯度目标更新策略；(5)引入自适应KL预算控制器，通过参考KL、有效样本量和奖励饱和信号调整熵约束来稳定策略更新。

Result: 在RTLLM v2.0（Nangate 45nm）上，PPA乘积几何平均降低65.1%，远超最强冻结策略基线（26.1%）；在XuanTie C910 FPU LZA单元（Sky130）上实现59.4%的ADP降低；消融实验验证了策略适应、状态重用和KL预算控制三个组件各自有效。

Conclusion: 测试时训练结合可执行的EDA反馈能够推动LLM的RTL生成从功能正确性向物理优化的硬件设计迈进，为LLM辅助硬件设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in generating
  functionally correct register-transfer-level (RTL) hardware designs.
  Recent systems improve further through EDA-integrated reinforcement
  learning with syntax, simulation, and PPA rewards, but train a general
  RTL generator before deployment while test-time approaches search with
  a frozen policy. We instead perform reinforcement learning at test time,
  allowing the LLM policy to adapt to executable EDA feedback for the
  specific RTL problem at hand. We propose TTT-RTL, to our knowledge the
  first per-design test-time training framework that closes the loop
  between an LLM policy and an EDA pipeline for RTL optimization. TTT-RTL
  samples candidate implementations, verifies them through syntax checking
  and simulation, scores valid designs using synthesis-derived PPA product,
  reuses high-reward variants through a PUCT-indexed design-state pool,
  and updates the policy with an entropic policy-gradient objective. To
  stabilize policy updates under sparse or plateaued rewards, we introduce
  an adaptive KL-budget controller that adjusts the entropy constraint
  using reference KL, effective sample size, and reward saturation signals.
  On RTLLM v2.0 under Nangate 45nm, TTT-RTL reduces the geometric-mean
  PPA product by 65.1% over the reference, outperforming the strongest
  published frozen-policy agent baseline at 26.1%. On an industrial
  XuanTie C910 FPU leading-zero-anticipation unit under Sky130, TTT-RTL
  achieves a 59.4% ADP reduction, and ablations confirm that policy
  adaptation, state reuse, and KL-budget control each contribute. These
  results suggest that test-time training with executable EDA feedback can
  move LLM-based RTL generation beyond functional correctness toward
  physically optimized hardware.

</details>


### [144] [Flash-WAM: Modality-Aware Distillation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05254)
*Arman Akbari,Ci Zhang,Arash Akbari,Lin Zhao,Yixiao Chen,Weiwei Chen,Xuan Zhang,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flash-WAM提出了一种模态感知的步骤蒸馏框架，通过为视频和动作流分别设计不同的参数化方法（线性梯度缩放+方差保持），将联合视频-动作世界模型的推理压缩到单步，实现23倍加速（从8.1秒降至348毫秒），在保留任务成功率的同时支持实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型(WAMs)需要大量去噪步骤，导致延迟过高无法用于实时控制。步骤蒸馏是自然解决方案，但现成方法无法处理视频和动作流使用不同SNR偏移噪声调度、具有显著不同边际噪声分布的非对称性问题。

Method: 提出Flash-WAM，基于一致性蒸馏的模态感知步骤蒸馏框架。通过结构分析一致性函数族，为动作流的低噪声 regime 选择线性梯度缩放参数化，为视频流的高噪声 regime 选择方差保持参数化，使每模态仅需单步推理。

Result: 在LingBot-VA上压缩到单步；在RoboTwin 2.0上延迟从8.1秒降至348毫秒（23倍加速）；模拟基准保留性能（RoboTwin 2.0: 85.5%, LIBERO: 95.7%）；真实世界Unitree G1人形机器人上恢复60%平均性能（朴素一致性蒸馏仅24%）。

Conclusion: Flash-WAM通过模态感知的蒸馏策略有效解决了联合视频-动作模型中的非对称噪声问题，实现了实时推理能力，同时保持了任务成功率，为世界模型在机器人实时控制中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and robot actions through iterative diffusion, achieving strong performance on manipulation benchmarks but requiring tens of denoising steps, a cost that precludes real-time control. Step distillation has emerged as the natural remedy, but off-the-shelf methods break down in the joint video-action setting because video and action streams use different SNR-shifted noise schedules and reach training with substantially different marginal noise distributions, an asymmetry that single-modality distillation methods cannot accommodate. We introduce \textbf{Flash-WAM}, a modality-aware step-distillation framework inspired by consistency distillation that selects the consistency function for each modality to match its noise regime: a linear-gradient-scaling parametrization for the action stream's low-noise regime, paired with a variance-preserving parametrization for the video stream's high-noise regime, grounded in a structural analysis of the consistency-function family that characterizes the achievable gradient scaling under the consistency boundary condition. Instantiated on LingBot-VA, Flash-WAM compresses inference to a single step in each modality. On RoboTwin 2.0, this reduces per-chunk latency from $8.1$ seconds to $348$ ms on NVIDIA L40S, a $23{\times}$ speedup that enables real-time inference. Flash-WAM preserves task success on simulation benchmarks ($85.5\%$ RoboTwin 2.0, $95.7\%$ LIBERO) and substantially recovers real-world performance ($60\%$ average on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot), while naive consistency distillation drops to $24\%$ at the same step budget.

</details>


### [145] [Scaling Laws for Behavioral Foundation Models over User Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05257)
*Rickard Brüel Gabrielsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了行为基础模型（如推荐、支付、欺诈等场景）的缩放定律，发现：小嵌入器（约2%参数）在计算最优时占优；训练偏向数据密集型但随计算量增加趋近Chinchilla启发式；损失函数与排序指标的差异本身具有缩放特性；评估指标本身也是缩放定律的一部分，改变它可能改变最优训练方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型越来越多地用于行为序列建模（推荐、支付、欺诈等），但这些模型缺乏像语言模型那样由缩放定律提供的计算校准指导，亟需系统性研究行为基础模型在不同部署相关轴上的计算最优配置。

Method: 研究了两部分行为模型架构（特征事件嵌入器+解码器仅Transformer），在~600次真实交互数据运行（$10^{15}$-$10^{19}$ FLOPs）中，联合变化四个部署相关轴：两部分参数分配、关键批大小、模型/数据分配、冻结嵌入器后的负采样数量。

Result: 小嵌入器（约2%参数）在各类计算预算下计算最优；计算最优训练在低计算量时相对文本更偏向数据密集，但随计算增加$D/N$比率趋近Chinchilla启发式；损失与排序指标的差异具有缩放特性；评估指标的改变会改变计算最优配方。

Conclusion: 在行为基础模型中，评估指标本身是缩放定律的一部分，改变评估指标可能改变计算最优的训练方案；负采样方面，更大预算偏好更多负样本，$10^{19}$ FLOPs时限制因素转为候选轴内存而非FLOPs。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly trained on sequences of user actions in recommendation, payments, fraud, and commerce, but these models still lack the kind of compute calibration that scaling laws provide for language models. We study a common two-part behavioral-model architecture: a feature-based event embedder maps each multi-modal item to a vector, and a decoder-only transformer predicts the next event from the resulting sequence. Across roughly 600 runs on real interaction data, spanning $10^{15}$-$10^{19}$ training FLOPs, we jointly vary four deployment-relevant axes: the two-part parameter split, critical batch size, model/data allocation, and the number of sampled negatives used after freezing the embedder. A small embedder ($s^{\star}\!\approx\!2\%$ of parameters) is compute-optimal at every budget we test because embedder parameters are both more expensive per step and exposed to far more repeated items than contextualizer parameters. Compute-optimal training is data-heavy relative to text at low compute, but its $D/N$ ratio moves toward the Chinchilla heuristic as compute increases. The sampled training objective and deployed ranking metrics disagree in ways that themselves scale: critical batch size, optimal negative count after freezing, and the agreement between loss and ranking quality all shift with compute and with the chosen evaluation metric. For negative sampling, larger budgets increasingly prefer more negatives; by $10^{19}$ FLOPs the active constraint is candidate-axis memory rather than FLOPs. In behavioral foundation models, the evaluation metric is therefore part of the scaling law: changing it can change the compute-optimal recipe.

</details>


### [146] [Policy-Conditioned Counterfactual Credit for Verifiable Reinforcement Learning of Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05263)
*Renwei Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CVT-RL算法，通过反事实贡献估计、干预有效性门控和可验证约束，显著提升长程语言智能体的可靠性和任务成功率，降低漏洞利用行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可验证奖励强化学习方法中，长程语言智能体存在学习未经验证的证据链、信念漂移和利用终端检查的捷径行为等问题。现有过程奖励大多是相关性的，无法估计步骤是否真正贡献于最终的可验证成功。

Method: 提出CVT-RL算法，包含：(1) 密集可验证奖励；(2) 干预有效性门控；(3) 策略条件反事实贡献(PCCC)估计器，使用删除、语义替换、证据替换、工具输出扰动四种控制干预，从冻结参考策略采样延续，采用选择调整的双重稳健估计器增强优势；(4) 信念控制使用仅前缀可观察标签；(5) 增广拉格朗日方法约束无根据主张、跳过验证、工具篡改和不安全调用。

Result: 在长上下文QA、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和网页/工具任务上，CVT-RL将平均任务成功率从非因果RL的71.8%和信息匹配基线75.4%提升至78.9%；证据F1从78.9提升至82.8；测量到的漏洞利用从7.2%降至3.9%；人工审计漏洞利用率为4.6%（基线8.1%），自适应检测规避攻击下漏洞利用仅升至7.1%。所有主要指标经Holm校正后p<0.01。

Conclusion: 精心设计的反事实信用分配、有效性门控、诊断和可验证约束，为更可靠的长程语言智能体强化学习提供了可复现的路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves reasoning and tool use, yet long-horizon language agents still learn unsupported evidence chains, belief drift, and shortcut actions that satisfy terminal checks. Existing process rewards are mostly correlational: they reward retrieval-, reflection-, or verification-like steps without estimating whether the step contributes to final verified success under a specified intervention. We propose CVT-RL, a constrained policy-gradient algorithm with dense verifiable rewards, intervention-validity gating, and a policy-conditioned counterfactual contribution (PCCC) estimator. Deletion, semantic substitution, evidence substitution, and tool-output perturbation define separate controlled interventions; continuations are sampled from a frozen reference policy, and a selection-adjusted doubly robust estimator augments the advantage. Belief control uses only prefix-observable labels, while an augmented Lagrangian constrains unsupported claims, skipped verification, tool tampering, and unsafe calls. On long-context QA, ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and web/tool tasks, CVT-RL improves average task success from 71.8% for compute-matched non-causal RL and 75.4% for an information-matched counterfactual-process baseline to 78.9%, improves evidence F1 from 78.9 to 82.8 over the information-matched baseline, and reduces measured hacking from 7.2% to 3.9%.
